# Quadraticity of linear combinations of three mutually commuting {α_i,β_i}-quadratic matrices — generic parameters
# Left-hand sides (eigenvalue patterns λ; one representative per unit-group orbit):
#   λ1 = (α1,α2,α3)
#   λ2 = (β1,α2,α3)
#   λ3 = (α1,β2,α3)
#   λ4 = (β1,β2,α3)
#   λ5 = (α1,α2,β3)
#   λ6 = (β1,α2,β3)
#   λ7 = (α1,β2,β3)
#   λ8 = (β1,β2,β3)
# A system is a set of patterns {λ…}; its solutions are the (c1,…,c3) with λ·c ∈ σ(X) for every λ in the set,
# all c_i ≠ 0.  Listed systems are closed (they contain every pattern satisfied by all their solutions).
# Groups = orbits under permutations of matrices with equal spectra and unit scalings X_i → uX_i (u·σ(X_i) = σ(X_i)).
# Groups whose representative has a zero column (some X_i = 0) are marked [zero column].
# 98 groups
